## Description

Stuck at piano? Here's how you'll be playing a song you love within 90 days confidently, for people you love: https://go.pianosso.com/v0

Play your first song in 1 hour. In this video, you're going to learn how to play piano from scratch with this comprehensive course. From reading sheet music to playing chords, this tutorial will guide you through every step with real-life case studies. Perfect for beginner adults looking to master the piano with easy tutorials. This is a complete piano roadmap no matter your musical goals on the piano. Classical music, pop music, jazz music, this will set you up for success. My objective is to make my free content better than the paid courses out there while also giving you unmatched support, a lacking resource in the online piano education space.
